<div className="bible-chapter" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-lang="en" data-translation="nmv" data-translation-id="eng_nmv" data-translation-label="NMV" data-book="LUK" data-book-slug="luke" data-book-name="Luke" data-chapter="1" data-verses="1151">

<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Acts 1:1–3; 2 Peter 1:16–19; John 20:31"> Forasmuch as many have taken in hand to set forth in order a declaration of those things which are most surely believed among us,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="John 15:27; Acts 26:16; Hebrews 2:3"> Even as they delivered them unto us, which from the beginning were eyewitnesses, and ministers of the word;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Acts 1:1; Acts 26:25; Acts 11:4"> It seemed good to me also, having had perfect understanding of all things from the very first, to write unto youi in order, most excellent Theophilus &#91;friend of God&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="John 20:31; Acts 18:25; 1 Corinthians 14:19"> That youi might know the certainty of those things, wherein youi have been instructed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="1 Chronicles 24:10; Gosel 2:1; Nehemiah 12:4"> There was in the days of Herod &#91;heroic&#93;, the king of Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93;, a certain priest named Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93;, of the course of Aviyah &#91;Yah is my father&#93;: and his wife &#91;was&#93; of the daughters of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and her name &#91;was&#93; Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 7:1; Genesis 17:1; Philippians 2:15"> And they were both righteous before God-Theos, walking in all the commandments and ordinances of the Lord - Kurios blameless.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Hebrews 11:11; Genesis 18:11; 1 Samuel 1:2"> And they had no child, because that Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93; was barren, and they both were &#91;now&#93; well stricken in years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="2 Chronicles 8:14; 1 Chronicles 24:19; 2 Chronicles 31:2"> And it came to pass, that while he executed the priest’s office before God-Theos in the order of his course,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Chronicles 29:11; Exodus 30:7–8; 1 Chronicles 23:13"> According to the custom of the priest’s office, his lot was to burn incense when he went into the temple of the Lord - Kurios.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Leviticus 16:17; Revelation 8:3; Hebrews 4:14"> And the whole multitude of the people were praying without at the time of incense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Exodus 40:26–27; Acts 10:3–4; Judges 13:3"> And there appeared unto him an angel of the Lord - Kurios standing on the right side of the altar of incense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Judges 13:22; Judges 6:22; Luke 2:9–10"> And when Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93; saw &#91;him&#93;, he was troubled, and fear fell upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:21; Acts 10:31; Genesis 25:21"> But the angel said unto him, Fear not, Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93;: for youri prayer is heard; and youri wife Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93; shall bear youi a son, and youi shall call his name Yochanan &#91;Yehovah has graced&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Luke 1:58; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:24; Genesis 21:6"> And youi shall have joy and gladness; and many shall rejoice at his birth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:5; Numbers 6:2–4; Luke 7:33"> For he shall be great in the sight of the Lord - Kurios, and shall drink neither wine nor strong drink; and he shall be filled with the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93;, even from his mother’s womb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Isaiah 40:3–5; Gosel 21:32; Malachi 3:1"> And many of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93; shall he turn to the Lord - Kurios their God-Theos.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Malachi 4:5–6; Gosel 11:14; Luke 1:76"> And he shall go before him in the Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93; and power of Eliyahu &#91;my God is Yehovah&#93;, to turn the hearts of the fathers to the children, and the disobedient to the wisdom of the just; to make ready a people prepared for the Lord - Kurios.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 38:22; Genesis 17:17; Luke 1:7"> And Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93; said unto the angel, Whereby shall I know this? for I am an old man, and my wife well stricken in years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Luke 1:26; Gosel 18:10; Daniel 9:21–23"> And the angel answering said unto him, I am Gabriel, that stand in the presence of God-Theos; and am sent to speak unto youi, and to show youi these glad tidings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:26; Ezekiel 24:27; Luke 1:22"> And, behold, youi shall be dumb, and not able to speak, until the day that these things shall be performed, because youi believe not my words, which shall be fulfilled in their season.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 6:23–27"> And the people waited for Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93;, and marvelled that he tarried so long in the temple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Luke 1:62; John 13:24; Acts 21:40"> And when he came out, he could not speak unto them: and they perceived that he had seen a vision in the temple: for he beckoned unto them, and remained speechless.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Chronicles 9:25; 2 Kings 11:5–7"> And it came to pass, that, as soon as the days of his ministration (service; ministry) were accomplished, he departed to his own house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And after those days his wife Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93; conceived, and hid herself five months, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Genesis 30:22–23; Isaiah 54:1–4; Genesis 21:1–2"> Thus has the Lord - Kurios dealt with me in the days wherein he looked on &#91;me&#93;, to take away my reproach (disgrace; shame) among men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Gosel 2:23; Luke 1:19; John 7:41"> And in the sixth month the angel Gabriel was sent from God-Theos unto a city of Galil &#91;circuit, district&#93;, named Natzeret &#91;one separated&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 1:18; Isaiah 7:14; Gosel 1:16"> To a virgin espoused to a man whose name was Yosef &#91;God will add&#93;, of the house of David &#91;Beloved&#93;; and the virgin’s name &#91;was&#93; Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Judges 6:12; Daniel 10:19; Luke 1:30"> And the angel came in unto her, and said, Hail, &#91;youi that are&#93; highly favoured, the Lord - Kurios &#91;is&#93; with youi: Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; youi among women.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Acts 10:17; Luke 1:12; Acts 10:4"> And when she saw &#91;him&#93;, she was troubled at his saying, and cast in her mind what manner of Greeting this should be.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Luke 1:13; Romans 8:31; Luke 12:32"> And the angel said unto her, Fear not, Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93;: for youi have found favour with God-Theos.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Isaiah 7:14; Gosel 1:21; Gosel 1:25"> And, behold, youi shall conceive in youri womb, and bring forth a son, and shall call his name YESHUA (GOD SAVES).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Isaiah 9:6–7; Isaiah 16:5; 2 Samuel 7:11–13"> He shall be great, and shall be called the Son of the Highest: and the Lord - Kurios God-Theos shall give unto him the throne of his father David &#91;Beloved&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Daniel 2:44; Hebrews 1:8; Daniel 7:13–14"> And he shall reign over the house of Ya’akov &#91;He who grabs onto the heal of&#93; &#91;Heal of God&#93; forever; and of his kingdom there shall be no end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Judges 13:8–12; Acts 9:6"> Then said Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; unto the angel, How shall this be, seeing I know not a man?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Gosel 1:18; John 1:49; Gosel 1:20"> And the angel answered and said unto her, The Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93; shall come upon youi, and the power of the Highest shall overshadow youi: therefore also that holy thing which shall be born of youi shall be called the Son of God-Theos.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And, behold, youri cousin Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93;, she has also conceived a son in her old age: and this is the sixth month with her, who was called barren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Gosel 19:26; Jeremiah 32:27; Mark 10:27"> For with God-Theos nothing shall be impossible.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Romans 4:20–21; Tehillim - Psalms 119:38; Tehillim - Psalms 116:16"> And Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; said, Behold the female slave of the Lord - Kurios; be it unto me according to youri word. And the angel departed from her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Luke 1:65; Joshua 15:48–59; Joshua 10:40"> And Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; arose in those days, and went into the hill country with haste, into a city of Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And entered into the house of Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93;, and saluted Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Luke 1:67; Acts 4:8; Luke 1:44"> And it came to pass, that, when Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93; heard the Greeting of Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93;, the babe leaped in her womb; and Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93; was filled with the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Luke 1:28; Judges 5:24; Genesis 22:18"> And she spoke out with a loud voice, and said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; youi among women, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; the fruit of youri womb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="John 20:28; John 13:13; Luke 2:11"> And from what place &#91;is&#93; this to me, that the mother of my Lord - Kurios should come to me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> For, lo, as soon as the voice of youri Greeting sounded in my ears, the babe leaped in my womb for joy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="John 20:29; John 11:40; Luke 11:27–28"> And Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; she that believed: for there shall be a performance of those things which were told her from the Lord - Kurios.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:1–10; Tehillim - Psalms 34:2–3; Tehillim - Psalms 35:9"> And Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; said, My soul does magnify the Lord - Kurios,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 35:9; 1 Timothy 2:3; Habakkuk 3:18"> And my Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93; has rejoiced in God-Theos my Saviour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 138:6; Malachi 3:12; 1 Corinthians 1:26–28"> For he has regarded the low estate of his handmaiden: for, behold, from from this time forth (from now on) all generations shall call me Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 126:2–3; Tehillim - Psalms 111:9; Ephesians 3:20"> For he that is mighty has done to me great things; and holy &#91;is&#93; his name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 103:17–18; Exodus 20:6; Genesis 17:7"> And his mercy &#91;is&#93; on them that fear him from generation to generation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 98:1; Isaiah 52:10; 1 Samuel 2:3–4"> He has showed strength with his arm; he has scattered the proud in the imagination of their hearts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52" data-xref="Ezekiel 17:24; Jacob 4:10; Tehillim - Psalms 107:40–41"> He has put down the mighty from &#91;their&#93; seats, and exalted them of low degree.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 107:8–9; Luke 6:21; Tehillim - Psalms 34:10"> He has filled the hungry with good things; and the rich he has sent empty away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 98:3; Micah 7:20; Isaiah 44:21"> He has helped his slave Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93;, in remembrance of &#91;his&#93; mercy;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:6–10; Genesis 17:19; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–17"> As he spoke to our fathers, to Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, and to his seed forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> And Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; abode with her about three months, and returned to her own house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57" data-xref="Luke 1:13; Genesis 21:2–3; Luke 2:6–7"> Now Elisheva &#91;the oath of God&#93;’s full time came that she should be delivered; and she brought forth a son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58" data-xref="Luke 1:25; Luke 1:14; Romans 12:15"> And her neighbours and her cousins heard how the Lord - Kurios had showed great mercy upon her; and they rejoiced with her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59" data-xref="Genesis 17:12; Leviticus 12:3; Philippians 3:5"> And it came to pass, that on the eighth day they came to circumcise the child; and they called him Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93;, after the name of his father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60" data-xref="Luke 1:13; 2 Samuel 12:25; Luke 1:63"> And his mother answered and said, Not &#91;so&#93;; but he shall be called Yochanan &#91;Yehovah has graced&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> And they said unto her, There is none of youri family that is called by this name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62" data-xref="Luke 1:22"> And they made signs to his father, how he would have him called.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63" data-xref="Luke 1:60; Isaiah 30:8; Luke 1:13"> And he asked for a writing table, and wrote, saying, His name is Yochanan &#91;Yehovah has graced&#93;. And they marvelled all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v64" data-v="64" data-xref="Luke 1:20; Exodus 4:15–16; Ezekiel 33:22"> And his mouth was opened immediately, and his tongue &#91;loosed&#93;, and he spoke, and praised God-Theos.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v65" data-v="65" data-xref="Luke 1:39; Acts 5:11; Luke 7:16"> And fear came on all that dwelt round about them: and all these sayings were noised abroad throughout all the hill country of Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v66" data-v="66" data-xref="Acts 11:21; Luke 2:19; Luke 2:51"> And all they that heard &#91;them&#93; laid &#91;them&#93; up in their hearts, saying, What manner of child shall this be! And the hand of the Lord - Kurios was with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v67" data-v="67" data-xref="Joel 2:28; Luke 1:41; Luke 1:15"> And his father Z’kharyah &#91;remembered by Yehovah&#93; was filled with the Holy Spirit &#91;Ruach HaKodesh&#93;, and prophesied, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v68" data-v="68" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 106:48; Luke 7:16; Tehillim - Psalms 41:13"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the Lord - Kurios God-Theos of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93;; for he has visited and redeemed his people,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v69" data-v="69" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 18:2; Ezekiel 29:21; 2 Samuel 22:3"> And has raised up an horn of salvation for us in the house of his slave David &#91;Beloved&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v70" data-v="70" data-xref="Acts 3:21–24; Romans 1:2; 2 Peter 1:21"> As he spoke by the mouth of his holy prophets, which have been since the world began:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v71" data-v="71" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 106:10; Jeremiah 23:6; Zephaniah 3:15–20"> That we should be saved from our enemies, and from the hand of all that hate us;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v72" data-v="72" data-xref="Hebrews 6:13–18; Micah 7:20; Tehillim - Psalms 106:45"> To finish the mercy &#91;promised&#93; to our fathers, and to remember his holy covenant;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v73" data-v="73" data-xref="Genesis 22:16–18; Tehillim - Psalms 105:9; Deuteronomy 7:8"> The oath which he swore to our father Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v74" data-v="74" data-xref="2 Timothy 1:7; Zephaniah 3:15–17; Isaiah 54:13–14"> That he would grant unto us, that we being delivered out of the hand of our enemies might serve him without fear,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v75" data-v="75" data-xref="Ephesians 4:24; Ephesians 2:10; Titus 2:11–14"> In holiness and righteousness before him, all the days of our life.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v76" data-v="76" data-xref="Malachi 3:1; Isaiah 40:3–5; Mark 1:2–3"> And youri, child, shall be called the prophet of the Highest: for youi shall go before the face of the Lord - Kurios to prepare his ways;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v77" data-v="77" data-xref="Acts 5:31; Acts 2:38; Acts 10:43"> To give knowledge of salvation unto his people by the forgiveness (pardon) of their sins,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v78" data-v="78" data-xref="Malachi 4:2; 2 Peter 1:19; Revelation 22:16"> Through the tender mercy of our God-Theos; whereby the Dayspring (sunrise) from on high has visited us,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v79" data-v="79" data-xref="Isaiah 9:2; Gosel 4:16; Acts 26:18"> To give light to them that sit in darkness and &#91;in&#93; the shadow of death, to guide our feet into the way of peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v80" data-v="80" data-xref="Luke 2:40; Mark 1:3–4; Luke 2:52"> And the child grew, and waxed strong in Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93;, and was in the deserts till the day of his showing unto Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Acts 1:1–3; 2 Peter 1:16–19; John 20:31"> And it came to pass in those days, that there went out a decree from Caesar &#91;venerable&#93; Augustus, that all the world should be taxed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="John 15:27; Acts 26:16; Hebrews 2:3"> &#91;And&#93; this taxing was first made when Quirinius &#91;warrior&#93; was governor of Aram &#91;exalted&#93; (Syria &#91;etymology is uncertain&#93;)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Acts 1:1; Acts 26:25; Acts 11:4"> And all went to be taxed, every one into his own city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="John 20:31; Acts 18:25; 1 Corinthians 14:19"> And Yosef &#91;God will add&#93; also went up from Galil &#91;circuit, district&#93;, out of the city of Natzeret &#91;one separated&#93;, into Y’hudah &#91;praised&#93;, unto the city of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, which is called Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93;; (because he was of the house and lineage of David &#91;Beloved&#93;)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Leviticus 16:17; Revelation 8:3; Hebrews 4:14"> It was Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; Magdala &#91;a tower&#93;, and Yochanan &#91;gift of God&#93;, and Miryam &#91;waters of strength&#93; &#91;the mother&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;heal-grabber&#93;, and other &#91;women that were&#93; with them, which told these things unto the apostles.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Exodus 40:26–27; Acts 10:3–4; Judges 13:3"> And their words seemed to them as idle tales, and they believed them not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Judges 13:22; Judges 6:22; Luke 2:9–10"> Then arose Kefa &#91;pebble&#93;, and ran unto the sepulchre; and stooping down, he beheld the linen clothes laid by themselves, and departed, wondering in himself at that which was come to pass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:21; Acts 10:31; Genesis 25:21"> And, behold, two of them went that same day to a village called Emmaus, which was from Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; &#91;about&#93; threescore furlongs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Luke 1:58; Mishlei - Proverbs 23:24; Genesis 21:6"> And they talked together of all these things which had happened.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Jeremiah 1:5; Numbers 6:2–4; Luke 7:33"> And it came to pass, that, while they communed &#91;together&#93; and reasoned, Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93; himself drew near, and went with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Isaiah 40:3–5; Gosel 21:32; Malachi 3:1"> But their eyes were held that they should not know him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Malachi 4:5–6; Gosel 11:14; Luke 1:76"> And he said unto them, What manner of communications &#91;are&#93; these that you2f have one to another, as you2f walk, and are sad?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Isaiah 38:22; Genesis 17:17; Luke 1:7"> And the one of them, whose name was Cleopas, answering said unto him, Are youi only a stranger in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and have not known the things which are come to pass there in these days?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Luke 1:26; Gosel 18:10; Daniel 9:21–23"> And he said unto them, What things? And they said unto him, Concerning Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93; of Natzeret &#91;one separated&#93;, which was a prophet mighty in deed and word before God-Theos and all the people:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:26; Ezekiel 24:27; Luke 1:22"> And how the chief priests and our rulers delivered him to be condemned to death, and have crucified him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 6:23–27"> But we trusted that it had been he which should have redeemed Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heal Of God&#93;: and beside all this, to day is the third day since these things were done.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Luke 1:62; John 13:24; Acts 21:40"> Yes, and certain women also of our company made us astonished, which were early at the sepulchre;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Chronicles 9:25; 2 Kings 11:5–7"> And when they found not his body, they came, saying, that they had also seen a vision of angels, which said that he was alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And certain of them which were with us went to the sepulchre, and found &#91;it&#93; even so as the women had said: but him they saw not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Genesis 30:22–23; Isaiah 54:1–4; Genesis 21:1–2"> Then he said unto them, O fools, and slow of heart to believe all that the prophets have spoken:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Gosel 2:23; Luke 1:19; John 7:41"> Ought not Moshiach &#91;Messiah&#93; to have suffered these things, and to enter into his glory?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 1:18; Isaiah 7:14; Gosel 1:16"> And beginning at Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and all the prophets, he expounded unto them in all the scriptures the things concerning himself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Judges 6:12; Daniel 10:19; Luke 1:30"> And they drew near unto the village, to which they went: and he made as though he would have gone further.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Acts 10:17; Luke 1:12; Acts 10:4"> But they constrained him, saying, Abide with us: for it is toward evening, and the day is far spent. And he went in to wait with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Luke 1:13; Romans 8:31; Luke 12:32"> And it came to pass, as he sat at meat with them, he took bread, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;it&#93;, and broke, and gave to them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Isaiah 7:14; Gosel 1:21; Gosel 1:25"> And their eyes were opened, and they knew him; and he vanished out of their sight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Isaiah 9:6–7; Isaiah 16:5; 2 Samuel 7:11–13"> And they said one to another, Did not our heart burn within us, while he talked with us by the way, and while he opened to us the scriptures?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Daniel 2:44; Hebrews 1:8; Daniel 7:13–14"> And they rose up the same hour, and returned to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and found the eleven gathered together, and them that were with them,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Judges 13:8–12; Acts 9:6"> Saying, The Lord - Kurios is risen indeed, and has appeared to Shim’on &#91;he has heard&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Gosel 1:18; John 1:49; Gosel 1:20"> And they told what things &#91;were done&#93; in the way, and how he was known of them in breaking of bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And as they thus spoke, Yeshua &#91;God is Salvation&#93; himself stood in the midst of them, and says unto them, Peace &#91;be&#93; unto you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Gosel 19:26; Jeremiah 32:27; Mark 10:27"> But they were terrified and affrighted, and supposed that they had seen a Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Romans 4:20–21; Tehillim - Psalms 119:38; Tehillim - Psalms 116:16"> And he said unto them, Why are you2f troubled? and why do thoughts arise in your hearts?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Luke 1:65; Joshua 15:48–59; Joshua 10:40"> Behold my hands and my feet, that it is I myself: handle me, and see; for a Spirit &#91;Ruach&#93; has not flesh and bones, as you2f see me have.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And when he had thus spoken, he showed them &#91;his&#93; hands and &#91;his&#93; feet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Luke 1:67; Acts 4:8; Luke 1:44"> And while they yet believed not for joy, and wondered, he said unto them, Have you2f here any meat?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Luke 1:28; Judges 5:24; Genesis 22:18"> And they gave him a piece of a broiled fish, and of an honeycomb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="John 20:28; John 13:13; Luke 2:11"> And he took &#91;it&#93;, and did eat before them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And he said unto them, These &#91;are&#93; the words which I spoke unto you, while I was yet with you, that all things must be fulfilled, which were written in the law of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and &#91;in&#93; the prophets, and &#91;in&#93; the psalms, concerning me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="John 20:29; John 11:40; Luke 11:27–28"> Then opened he their understanding, that they might understand the scriptures,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:1–10; Tehillim - Psalms 34:2–3; Tehillim - Psalms 35:9"> And said unto them, Thus it is written, and thus it behoved Moshiach &#91;Messiah&#93; to suffer (allow, to let, permit), and to rise from the dead the third day:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 35:9; 1 Timothy 2:3; Habakkuk 3:18"> And that repentance and forgiveness (pardon) of sins should be preached in his name among all nations, beginning at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 138:6; Malachi 3:12; 1 Corinthians 1:26–28"> And you2f are witnesses of these things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 126:2–3; Tehillim - Psalms 111:9; Ephesians 3:20"> And, behold, I send the promise of my Father upon you: but wait you2f in the city of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, until you2f be endued with power from on high.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 103:17–18; Exodus 20:6; Genesis 17:7"> And he led them out as far as to Beit-Anyah &#91;house of figs&#93;, and he lifted up his hands, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 98:1; Isaiah 52:10; 1 Samuel 2:3–4"> And it came to pass, while he Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) them, he was parted from them, and carried up into heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52" data-xref="Ezekiel 17:24; Jacob 4:10; Tehillim - Psalms 107:40–41"> And they worshipped him, and returned to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; with great joy:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 107:8–9; Luke 6:21; Tehillim - Psalms 34:10"> And were continually in the temple, praising and blessing God-Theos. Amen.</span>
</div>
